## Corpus Check
- 4643 files · ~10,762,871 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 64923 nodes · 187430 edges · 1259 communities (1139 shown, 120 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7748 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0a515571`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
